THIRD-PARTY LICENSES FOR JANK!
========================================================================
Jank! consists of its own code (the crates `jank` and `psxcore`)
and of the open-source crates listed below. The Jank! crates use
only the Rust standard library for modeling, rasterizing, image
and video codecs and the project format.

Each crate is used under the licence shown. Where a crate offers
a choice ("A OR B"), the permissive option marked 'used under'
applies. No crate is used under GPL or AGPL terms.

Crates: 322

DEFINITIONS
"Font Software" refers to the set of files released by the Copyright

as received under this licence.

"Modified Version" refers to any derivative made by adding to, deleting,
or substituting -- in part or in whole -- any of the components of the
Original Version, by changing formats or by porting the Font Software to
a new environment.

Butterfly model and texture (core/assets/butterfly.obj, butterfly_texture.png; demo/butterfly.jank) - supplied by the project owner
for use in Jank!. They are built into the program as the Butterfly primitive and carried inside the demo project.
The butterfly icon in the Add row, the demo's meadow, bark, needle and sky pictures are original work made for Jank! and generated
in code (core/examples/make_butterfly_demo.rs); no third-party pictures are included.
Splash pictures (app/assets/splash/) - supplied by the project owner for use as the start-up screens of Jank!.
